# Third-party notices

Data Analysis Bot (DAB) is built with the open-source packages listed here: every production dependency of its server (backend/) and its web app (frontend/), 403 packages in all, with the licence each declares and where its source code lives. The licence texts the packages ship follow, each distinct text printed once with every package that ships it. Development dependencies (build and test tools such as Vite, Tailwind CSS and Supertest) are not listed.

Fonts: IBM Plex Sans Thai and IBM Plex Mono, © IBM Corp., under the SIL Open Font License 1.1. The full licence is in LICENSES/OFL-1.1-IBM-Plex.txt, served at /licenses/OFL-1.1-IBM-Plex.txt.

Generated by `npm run licenses` from package-lock.json and the installed packages. Do not edit by hand: `npm run licenses:check` fails when this file is stale, or when a package's licence is outside the allow-list and has not been reviewed.

## Licences reviewed by hand

These packages declare a licence outside the allow-list (MIT, ISC, BSD-2-Clause, BSD-3-Clause, Apache-2.0, 0BSD, OFL-1.1, CC0-1.0, Unlicense, BlueOak-1.0.0, Python-2.0, CC-BY-4.0, or an OR choice that includes one of them), or none at all. Each was looked at, and the reason it ships is recorded in scripts/third-party-notices.mjs.

- buffers 0.1.1 - (none declared): Declares no licence and ships no licence file (0.1.1, 2011). Pulled in by exceljs → unzipper → binary, all MIT; its author also wrote binary (MIT) and chainsaw and traverse (MIT/X11) in the same period. No grant is on record, so the owner should confirm with upstream or replace the chain.
- chainsaw 0.1.0 - MIT/X11: MIT/X11 is the MIT licence under its older name; no licence file in the package (via exceljs → unzipper → binary).
- pako 1.0.11 - (MIT AND Zlib): Both permissive: MIT for pako's own code, the zlib licence for the parts ported from zlib, whose notice stays in pako's source files. Its LICENSE file (MIT) is below.
- png-js 1.1.0 - (none declared): No licence field in package.json, but its LICENSE file is the MIT licence (Devon Govett). Used by pdfkit.
- traverse 0.3.9 - MIT/X11: MIT/X11 is the MIT licence under its older name; its LICENSE file is the MIT text.
- victory-vendor 36.9.2 - MIT AND ISC: Both terms are in the allow-list: MIT for Victory, ISC for the d3 modules it bundles (used by recharts).

### Packages that ship no licence file

These packages declare a licence in package.json but include no licence text. The author named in the package is shown.

- @libsql/client 0.14.0 - MIT - https://github.com/libsql/libsql-client-ts
- @libsql/core 0.14.0 - MIT - https://github.com/libsql/libsql-client-ts
- @libsql/isomorphic-fetch 0.3.1 - MIT - https://github.com/libsql/isomorphic-ts
- @libsql/isomorphic-ws 0.1.5 - MIT - https://github.com/libsql/isomorphic-ts
- @swc/helpers 0.3.17 - MIT - 강동윤 - https://github.com/swc-project/swc
- binary 0.3.0 - MIT - James Halliday - https://github.com/substack/node-binary
- brotli 1.3.3 - MIT - Devon Govett - https://github.com/devongovett/brotli.js
- buffers 0.1.1 - (none declared) - James Halliday - https://github.com/substack/node-buffers
- chainsaw 0.1.0 - MIT/X11 - James Halliday - https://github.com/substack/node-chainsaw
- cookie-signature 1.0.7 - MIT - TJ Holowaychuk - https://github.com/visionmedia/node-cookie-signature
- data-uri-to-buffer 4.0.1 - MIT - Nathan Rajlich - https://github.com/TooTallNate/node-data-uri-to-buffer
- dfa 1.2.0 - MIT - Devon Govett - https://github.com/devongovett/dfa
- fontkit 1.9.0 - MIT - Devon Govett - https://github.com/foliojs/fontkit
- isarray 1.0.0 - MIT - Julian Gruber - https://github.com/juliangruber/isarray
- pg-types 2.2.0 - MIT - Brian M. Carlson - https://github.com/brianc/node-pg-types
- pgpass 1.0.5 - MIT - Hannes Hörl - https://github.com/hoegaarden/pgpass
- promise-limit 2.7.0 - ISC - Tim Macfarlane - https://github.com/featurist/promise-limit
- saxes 5.0.1 - ISC - Louis-Dominique Dubeau - https://github.com/lddubeau/saxes
- tr46 0.0.3 - MIT - Sebastian Mayr - https://github.com/Sebmaster/tr46.js
- undici-types 5.26.5 - MIT - https://github.com/nodejs/undici
- victory-vendor 36.9.2 - MIT AND ISC - Formidable - https://github.com/FormidableLabs/victory

### Prebuilt platform binaries

Each machine installs only the binary for its own system, so these are described from package-lock.json. They ship no licence file; each comes from the same project as the package named.

- @libsql/darwin-arm64 0.4.7 - MIT - prebuilt binary for libsql
- @libsql/darwin-x64 0.4.7 - MIT - prebuilt binary for libsql
- @libsql/linux-arm64-gnu 0.4.7 - MIT - prebuilt binary for libsql
- @libsql/linux-arm64-musl 0.4.7 - MIT - prebuilt binary for libsql
- @libsql/linux-x64-gnu 0.4.7 - MIT - prebuilt binary for libsql
- @libsql/linux-x64-musl 0.4.7 - MIT - prebuilt binary for libsql
- @libsql/win32-x64-msvc 0.4.7 - MIT - prebuilt binary for libsql
